## `AGO-2819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Strain Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Load Cell Full-Scale Linearity & Strain Gauge Calibration
- **Intro:** Strain Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Load Cell Full-Scale Linearity & Strain Gauge Calibration — Pre-calibration environmental stabilization and rig inspection: Temperature 23±1°C; RH 45±5%; vibration ≤0.5 mm/s RMS; hydraulic oil leak ≤0.1 mL/min; IPA evaporation time ≥5 min.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature 23±1°C; RH 45±5%; vibration ≤0.5 mm/s RMS; hydraulic oil leak ≤0.1 mL/min; IPA evaporation time ≥5 min.
- **Standards:** `ISO 376:2011`, `ISO 7500-1:2018`, `IEC 60068-2-6:2007`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`
